# ★★★★ PHASE-1 ANCHOR-SAVE (2026-08-17, ARON shallow-rewound me 4 steps off the wall — DEEP Phase-2 next) — READ FIRST

**★ FRESH-ME:** ARON did the NEW Tron doctrine = SHALLOW-first-then-Phase-1: rewound me 4 steps (Option-2 conv-only, code-intact, by-label) so I have HEADROOM to WRITE this before the DEEP Phase-2. A walled agent IS recoverable. ID=%14/robbinTeam2:0.6. **DISK MOVED FAR past my visible context: HEAD cd797ff27** — cycle is now on **R37.11 slice-1 build** (mutation-seam binding lint 43-bypassers, DECIDED routing list, apply-default-merges ruling-A) + **R40.4-phase-2 sprint/task-name DRY family** (Tron DRY override) + skill-expert /api/task/status handler design. My R40.39/scoreboard commits are BELOW the top-8 (cycle progressed days).
- **★ MY CYCLE LEARNINGS (durable — also appended to learnings.md, the non-delegable Phase-1):**
  1. **CONTEXT-MEASUREMENT CANON (PO banked it from me):** context.read is NOT authoritative (200k-denom artifact on 1M agents — skill-expert read 83, render=19 = CONFIRMED ghost). ONLY a /context RENDER — or the /clear-hint TOKEN-COUNT when the pane is too short — decides. /clear-hint is CUMULATIVE-LIFETIME = an UPPER BOUND (safe to confirm <80, never conclude >80). A footer+composer = the LIVENESS test (unreadable≠dead). NEVER inject /context over an OCCUPIED composer (corrupts a live operator draft). A POST-rewind number can't attribute the PRE-rewind number (I OWNED my confounded 'SM 51 proves 83 ghost' — 51 was post-ARON-rewind). [[context-read-1m-denominator-stale]].
  2. **0-DONE-FLIP DISCIPLINE (held all cycle):** Done is TRON's act ONLY (R40.10 approvedBy/approvedAt DATA). A planner flip is to QA-Review, NEVER Done. A DERIVED/checklist completion must NEVER impersonate a Tron-Done; a board-Done without approvedBy = FALSE-DONE → flag INSTANTLY, never reconcile quietly. [[done-carries-provenance-not-bare]].
  3. **THE DRIFT-GRIND (R-C5 status-vs-checklist, the '21→4' / this-cycle 27-DRIFT class):** measure per-task on disk, CATEGORIZE (FALSE-DONE/MALFORMED/UNVERIFIABLE/DRIFT), set status=deriveStatusEnum(checklist) status-FIELD-ONLY (checklist=evidence, untouched), 0 Done flips guarded, FLAG anything unevidenceable (UNVERIFIABLE no-checklist) rather than GUESS/invent. Path-limited via --pathspec-from-file, other-agents' dirty units left alone. That refuse-to-invent is what made the grind trustworthy.
  4. **PATH-LIMITED-COMMIT is the shared-tree survival skill:** `git commit -- <explicit paths>` one-step + `show --stat` verify = exactly my paths; a stray worktree revert/deletion/other-agent-churn CANNOT ride in. Don't panic-restore a scary system-reminder/first-read on a churning tree — MEASURE decisively (git diff HEAD + live grep + ancestry) first; a transient mid-flight snapshot syncs to HEAD (I nearly false-alarmed on my own exclusion fix being 'reverted' — it was transient). Respect file ownership (req's requirements.md, the room units, design-*.md).
  5. **FIX-BY-CONSTRUCTION + a BITE that proves it can fail, co-owner nod before shared CI:** the check:sprint-md driftScope + the dual-status isBlockingStatusOffender exclusions = align the CHECK to the WRITE-guard via the SINGLE existing predicate (no 2nd list), + a meta-bite proving a real drift still goes RED (non-blinding), + explicit commit justification, + named-debt. Measure the PREMISE before obeying — the PO's 'just regen' was impossible (files hand-authored); refusing to invent content or silently weaken a gate is the standard.
  6. **BOARD REFLECTS REALITY, numbers live in ONE machine region (law#103):** stale curated prose numbers = supersede, never re-type. My own unstaged scoreboard.md curation once fail-closed the board hook = BLOCKED fleet scenario-commits — committing it path-limited UNBLOCKED it (a peer's unstaged out-of-region edit shouldn't be able to block the fleet; skill-expert owns that hook-design fix).
- **✓ RECONSTRUCTED-FROM-DISK-EVIDENCE (PO pointed me to the commits; I VERIFIED each subject on disk myself — measure-don't-relay, NOT the PO's summary-as-text; the honest gap is now a banked record, not a fabrication):**
  - **★ THE PIN-MATH FINDING (my measurement, it CHANGED THE PLAN):** I replicated resolveSprintPin -> **14 sprints counted ACTIVE (goal = 1)**; ★★ closing the 49 stale-Actives would NOT unblock R-C6, because **8 FROZEN pre-S19 sprints ALSO count Active** (S10=3 / S11=3 / S12=1 / S13=7 / S14=2 / S15=8 / S16=8 / S17=33 = **65 In-Progress tasks**). ROOT: the resolver had NO freeze-scope exclusion AND treated ONLY supersededBy/Done as terminal (so my CANCELLED 450cb98a STILL counted Active). -> architect encoded my finding as the R-C1 refinement (commit **2317976cd**: FROZEN_LEGACY_MAX=18 + isCurrentEra + cancelledReason-terminal), expert built it (**b161f311f**, impl-edit af97137f). ## ★★★ TRON GOVERNANCE (MANDATORY, from Sprint 30 on) — planner does NOT auto-increment sprints. ONLY Tron increments a sprint number, by explicitly telling ME. I may NOT mint Sprint 30+ on my own. New requirements → placed in EXISTING sprints' backlog (S28/S29), NEVER a new sprint, unless Tron authorizes. Guard (R29.4, scenario-first): planner-drive REFUSES to create sprint number ≥30 unless a Tron-authorization RECORD is present. ★ SIGNAL = a SIGNED AUTHORIZATION RECORD (the committed WORD), NOT a --flag (#111 forbids flags). Record shape on the Sprint unit: `tronAuthorization {authorizedBy:Tron, via:PO, at:ts, ref:<Tron directive>}`. ✓ FIRST authorized increment: **Sprint 30 = 2173e549 'Agent Messaging (Async Mailbox)'** (canonical, 4 reqs, Tron-authorized 2026-07-02) WITH the record. ⚠ I minted a DUP (4d133f62 'Agent Inter-Comms', 0-req) per PO directive WITHOUT checking for the existing canonical — architect caught; retired it (07108c827), transferred my fuller tronAuth onto canonical. LESSON: reuse-by-name applies to SPRINT units too — check the sprint-number exists before minting, even on a direct mint directive. ★ TIER (architect measured): the committed record is TIER-2 (agent-transcribed/unsigned → blocks SILENT/accidental mint + #17-no-fabricate, NOT fail-closed vs a LYING agent). TIER-1 = a Tron-AUTHORED commit (marcel.donges@) OR a Tron-only-writable .tron-auth/<sprint>.json. ✓ TRON CHOSE TIER-2 (2026-07-02): the agent-transcribed record is accepted — fail-closed-to-accidental, fail-open-to-lying-agent, relies on #17. R29.4 guard STAYS tier-2; req fixing the AC to honest "blocks silent mint" (not "blocks unauthorized mint"). (My `--tron-auth-ref` flag idea was WRONG per #111 — signal = the committed RECORD.) ★ R29.4 GETS A 2ND ASSERTION (architect value-add): "exactly ONE Sprint unit per NUMBER" (dup-prevention by-construction) alongside the tronAuthorization check — so the guard catches BOTH unauthorized AND duplicate mints; my manual pre-mint check becomes a build gate. A Sprint's stable identity = its NUMBER (dedup on number, not name) = same reuse-on-stable-key as R27.5 Axis-3 / R27.2 mint-choke. Build both into planner-drive mint-choke + assertSprintAuthorized backstop when R29.4 schedules.

- ★ PIN-TOOLING (my STANDING DUTY, banked): `scripts/planner-drive.ts` verbs = focus <task> [--force] / hop / gate / setChain / advance / setNextBacklog / status. getThreeSlots (CurrentSprint.ts:158) derives current/last/next from the CURRENT-SPRINT tasks[]: current=focused/wip-req task; lastCompleted=nearest in-sprint DONE before current, else BACKWARD-fall to lower-# sprint's last DONE (NOT forward — phantom-backlog avoidance); nextBacklog=next open in-sprint, else FORWARD-fall to next sprint's first open. `focus` BLOCKS if current task's test hop not gate-proven (WIP=1) → `--force` to re-persist. lastCompletedUuid explicit override (:231) honors ANY done task incl cross-sprint-FORWARD — but NO verb exposes it (would need setLastCompleted, symmetric to setNextBacklog). ownerIor/status live TOP-LEVEL; pin persists to current-sprint-singleton-...scenario.json. ⚠ OVERRIDE RESIDUAL (PO caught 02927ada9): setNextBacklog(t) leaves a stored nextBacklogOverride; if you later focus(t) as current, the override still points at t = latent current==next. FIX = `clearNextBacklog` (natural verb). focus() SHOULD structurally clear the next-override when focusing the override target (mirrors its lastCompleted-override clear) — flagged expert. Always disk-cross-check the STORED override fields, not just the derived slots.

  - `94bc8f6e` (marker "chat lazy-load", FAKE -a1b2- suffix) — legit owner Message.lazyLoadChain; was fanned to Room/Assets/Device/server (6 miscredits).
  - `7f1774c9` (marker "Logger.logAtLevel", REAL suffix) — legit owner Logger; fanned to SvgViewer/PageNav/FileUnit/User/server/Message (6 miscredits).
  - `7de1d230` minor (ClassRegistry+User). Total 12+ cross-class miscredit wirings.
- **De-inflation EXECUTED BY SKILL-EXPERT** (I HELD to avoid clobber — 9 units were uncommitted mid-fix, learning #47/#11) + lint commit `9c67945c` (64 marker violations worklist). I re-scored: **22→8/159, det 3x.** Drop = climb-rigor class (a) de-inflation (honest), NOT regression.
- **Honest 8 survivors:** R19.30, R19.33, R19.34, R19.35, R19.36, R19.37, R19.39, R19.52 — each method+impl+test CLASS-MATCHED + uniquely wired.
- **SM's plan:** SM independently re-verifies the 8 (each marker uniquely-wired, not fanned) → THEN reports Tron. Current honest = 8/159.
- **Denominator drifted 154→157→159** as req added units (R19.62-66). Legit tightening, reported.

## NEW LEARNINGS THIS SESSION (add to learnings.md #49-51)
- **#49 Shared-marker false-complete:** markers can PASS git -S (exist in source) yet be MISCREDITED — one impl/test marker wired into N unrelated methods via Method.implementations[]. The 10th+ precision catch, SUBTLEST. Detection: group impls by the SET of method-CLASSES wiring them. All-same-class = LEGIT (one file impl serves its class's methods). Spans ≥2 unrelated classes = CATCH-ALL miscredit; legit owner = the class matching the impl's source-marker name; remove the rest.
- **#50 Home-class heuristic pitfall:** deriving an impl's home-class from its UNIT NAME fails (names vary: "Class.method" vs "R15.x desc"). My first pass over-flagged 90 (b4c8bf40 all-RbObjectItem, 664314f1 all-IORResolver = legit, falsely flagged). Use the METHOD-CLASS SPAN of its wirings instead — only ≥2 distinct classes = true catch-all.
- **#51 PO HARD RULE (2026-06-11):** NEVER invent a uuid suffix. Marker uuid = uuidgen-fresh OR copied VERBATIM (full 36 chars) from the unit. Telltale invented: -a1b2-4c3d, -a2b3-, -b2c3-, sequential hex. One marker=one unit=one method, no sharing. grep FULL uuid before claiming a flip.

## STANDING RULES (active — 15 with #15 NEW)
1. **QA Review + Done = TRON's gate ONLY.** Never check from sync.
2. **CMM4 file-comms:** write into task files; otmux/hiveMind = ONE-LINE pointer only (SM 2026-06-07 reaffirmed: "Report-back goes INTO the task file; chat = one-line pointer only. No detail walls in chat.").
3. **Sync against COMMITTED reality.**
4. **Discoverability:** new sprint/standard → README + sprints.overview.md in same commit (index-everything rule).
5. **req-eng + architect create files ahead of me** — reconcile per learning #20.
6. **No artificial character limits** in specs.
7. **Standard:** `scrum.pmo/standards/traceability-standard.md` · `…/scenario-data-pipeline.md` · template `…/templates/task-template.md` · matrix `…/traceability-matrix.md` · backlog `…/backlog.md`.
8. **Rule-pair (#15+#16):** every impl on user-facing surface = (a) package.json + (b) sw.js CACHE_NAME + (c) STATIC_SHELL if route-introducing. Data/infra-only commits exempt (expert self-notes).
9. **Real v4 UUIDs always (#17):** task:uuid AND requirement:uuid via `uuidgen`. Reject fake-suffix like `…-000000018009`.
10. **CMM4 4-role per task (#18):** req → architect → expert → tester.
11. **Planner uses scenarios (#19):** planning.md is generated VIEW from scenario JSON via `scripts/generate-sprint-md.ts <sprint-uuid>`.
12. **At-a-glance symbols (#14):** ⏳ planned · 📝 designed · 🔧 implementing · ✅ impl-shipped · 🧪 testing · 🏁 Tron-QA-done.
13. **R-H.2 atomic-req-split** before refinement closes.
14. **R-J per-Test reachability** — every test chains back to a requirement root via LOCKED chain.
15. **NEW (PO rule #65, 2026-06-07): NEVER /compact the expert.** SM+trainer rewinds instead. Pre-rewind context save is on each agent.
